## sourcesQuery
SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, FALSE AS body_hit, NULL::text, NULL::text FROM sources s WHERE TRUE ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: I(0), I(0)
## countQuery
SELECT count(*)::int FROM sources s WHERE TRUE
  params: 
## findingsFeedQuery
SELECT f.citekey, s.title, f.claim, f.region_name, ST_X(f.geom), ST_Y(f.geom), lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, f.confidence, f.significance, s.doi, s.venue, s.source, s.tags, s.topics, EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM media_links m WHERE m.target_type = 'source' AND m.target_id = s.citekey AND m.role = 'cover') FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND TRUE ORDER BY lower(f.period) NULLS LAST, f.citekey, f.id LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: I(0), I(0)
## findingsCountQuery
SELECT count(*)::int FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND TRUE
  params: 
## selectionCountsQuery
SELECT (SELECT count(*)::int FROM sources s WHERE TRUE), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND TRUE), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM articles a WHERE TRUE), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM events e WHERE TRUE)
  params: 
## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: 
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND TRUE
  params: 
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: 
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND TRUE ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params:

# q · no scope
## where
(s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse)
## sourceKeys
SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse)
## articleWhere
EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article_links al WHERE al.src_slug = a.slug AND al.target_kind = 'source' AND al.target = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))))
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse)
## eventWhere
(e.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM attestations ea WHERE ea.target_type = 'event' AND ea.target_id = e.id AND ea.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))))))
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse)
## sourceWhere
(s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse)

## orderBySql
ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse)
## orderBySql cte
ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(collapse)
## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), I(0), I(0)
## countQuery
SELECT count(*)::int FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse)
## findingsFeedQuery
SELECT f.citekey, s.title, f.claim, f.region_name, ST_X(f.geom), ST_Y(f.geom), lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, f.confidence, f.significance, s.doi, s.venue, s.source, s.tags, s.topics, EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM media_links m WHERE m.target_type = 'source' AND m.target_id = s.citekey AND m.role = 'cover') FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY lower(f.period) NULLS LAST, f.citekey, f.id LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse), I(0), I(0)
## findingsCountQuery
SELECT count(*)::int FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse)
## selectionCountsQuery
SELECT (SELECT count(*)::int FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM articles a WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article_links al WHERE al.src_slug = a.slug AND al.target_kind = 'source' AND al.target = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))))), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM events e WHERE (e.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM attestations ea WHERE ea.target_type = 'event' AND ea.target_id = e.id AND ea.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))))))
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse)
## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse)
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse)
## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S(collapse), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?

## periodsQuery sources

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)

## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse)
## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S(collapse), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S(collapse), S(collapse), S(collapse)

## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S("sea peoples"), S("sea peoples"), S("sea peoples"), S("sea peoples"), S("sea peoples"), I(0), I(0)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey

## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S("sea peoples"), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S("sea peoples"), S("sea peoples"), S("sea peoples")

## sourcesQuery

AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?

## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(tin -islands), S(tin -islands), S(tin -islands), S(tin -islands), S(tin -islands)

## snippetsQuery

AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord

## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(tin or kings), S(tin or kings), S(tin or kings), S(tin or kings), S(tin or kings)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord

## orderBySql

  params: S(50% of_it)
## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), I(0), I(0)

## orderBySql

FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it)
## orderBySql cte

  params: S(50% of_it)
## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), I(0), I(0)

## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it)
## periodsQuery findings

  params: S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it)
## treeRowsQuery findings

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it)
## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S(50% of_it), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it), S(50% of_it)

## findingsCountQuery

  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples)
## selectionCountsQuery
SELECT (SELECT count(*)::int FROM sources s WHERE (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?)))), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)))), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM articles a WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article_links al WHERE al.src_slug = a.slug AND al.target_kind = 'source' AND al.target = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))))))), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM events e WHERE (e.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))))) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM attestations ea WHERE ea.target_type = 'event' AND ea.target_id = e.id AND ea.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))))))))
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples)
## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples)
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)))
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples)
# tags · no scope
## where
(EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?)))
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(steppe), S(steppe)
## sourceKeys

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?)))
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(steppe), S(steppe)
## articleWhere

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))))))
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(steppe), S(steppe)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?)))))))
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(steppe), S(steppe), S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(steppe), S(steppe)

## sourcesQuery
SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, FALSE AS body_hit, NULL::text, NULL::text FROM sources s WHERE (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(steppe), S(steppe), I(0), I(0)

## periodsQuery sources

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)

## periodsQuery findings

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) ## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(steppe), S(steppe)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(steppe), S(steppe)

## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(sea-peoples), S(sea-peoples), S(steppe), S(steppe)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey

ESCAPE '\') OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.authors) x WHERE x ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\')) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(%cline%), S(%anthony%)

## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(%Anatolia%)

## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(%Anatolia%)
## treeRowsQuery findings

ESCAPE '\' ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(%Anatolia%)

## treeRowsQuery sources

ESCAPE '\') fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(%zq\_land%)

ESCAPE '\' OR f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\')) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(%zq\_land%), S(%Crete%)
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\' OR f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\')
  params: S(%zq\_land%), S(%Crete%)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\' OR f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\')) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(%zq\_land%), S(%Crete%)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\' OR f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(%zq\_land%), S(%Crete%)
# places · scoped
## where

ESCAPE '\') OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f JOIN finding_places fp ON fp.finding_id = f.id JOIN place_names pn ON pn.place_id = fp.place_id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND pn.name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\')))
  params: A(List(zq_land, zq_tell, zq_cove)), S(%Crete%), S(%Crete%), S(%Crete%)

## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326)))
  params: D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326))) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)

# q and tag · no scope
## where
(s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?)))
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade)
## sourceKeys
SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?)))
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade)
## articleWhere
EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article_links al WHERE al.src_slug = a.slug AND al.target_kind = 'source' AND al.target = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))))))
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade)
## eventWhere
(e.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))))) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM attestations ea WHERE ea.target_type = 'event' AND ea.target_id = e.id AND ea.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?)))))))
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), S(tin), S(tin), S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade)
## sourceWhere
(s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(tin), S(tin)
## findingPlaceWhere
(EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)))
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade)
## findingPeriodWhere
TRUE
  params: 
## findingWhere
(EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)))
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade)
## orderBySql
ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(tin)
## orderBySql cte
ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(tin)
## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) ## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)))) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey

## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S(tin), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin)

## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), S(tin), I(0), I(0)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin)
## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S(tin), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin)

## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326)))
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326))) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)

## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326)))
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326)))) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND fp.matched > 0 AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND (f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326) OR f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326))) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(zq-trade), S(zq-trade), D(170.0), D(-25.0), D(180.0), D(-10.0), D(-180.0), D(-25.0), D(-170.0), D(-10.0)

## orderBySql
ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)
## orderBySql cte
ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(walls)
## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.authors) x WHERE x ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') AND ((EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.regions) x WHERE x ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND f.region_name ILIKE ? ## periodsQuery sources

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\' OR f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326))) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.authors) x WHERE x ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.topics) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) AND lower(s.status) IN (?, ?) AND (s.year IS NOT NULL AND s.year BETWEEN ? AND ?) AND s.title ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\' AND s.citekey IN (SELECT ref FROM goal_links WHERE goal_slug = ? AND kind = 'source') AND fp.matched > 0 AND (s.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]') OR fp.in_window > 0)

ESCAPE '\') AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326))) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ESCAPE '\' AND s.citekey IN (SELECT ref FROM goal_links WHERE goal_slug = ? AND kind = 'source') AND fp.matched > 0 AND (s.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]') OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey

## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S(walls), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)

## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) x WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND lower(x) = lower(?))) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.authors) x WHERE x ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') AND (s.citekey IN (SELECT f.citekey FROM findings f JOIN finding_places fp ON fp.finding_id = f.id WHERE fp.place_id = ANY(?::text[])) OR (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.regions) x WHERE x ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f JOIN finding_places fp ON fp.finding_id = f.id JOIN place_names pn ON pn.place_id = fp.place_id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND pn.name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\'))) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.topics) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) AND lower(s.status) IN (?, ?) AND (s.year IS NOT NULL AND s.year BETWEEN ? AND ?) AND (s.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]') OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND f.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]'))) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND f.geom IS NOT NULL AND f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326)) AND s.title ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\' AND s.citekey IN (SELECT ref FROM goal_links WHERE goal_slug = ? AND kind = 'source') ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(zq-walls), S(zq-walls), S(zq-oracle), S(zq-oracle), S(%zqauthor%), A(List(zq_tell)), S(%Zqcove%), S(%Zqcove%), S(%Zqcove%), S(bronze age), S(current), S(contested), L(1950), L(2020), L(-1600), L(-1100), L(-1600), L(-1100), D(30.0), D(36.0), D(40.0), D(42.0), S(%walls%), S(zq-goal), S(walls), I(0), I(0)
## countQuery

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.id IN (SELECT fp.finding_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.place_id = ANY(?::text[])) OR f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326))) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.authors) x WHERE x ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.topics) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) AND lower(s.status) IN (?, ?) AND (s.year IS NOT NULL AND s.year BETWEEN ? AND ?) AND s.title ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\' AND s.citekey IN (SELECT ref FROM goal_links WHERE goal_slug = ? AND kind = 'source') AND fp.matched > 0 AND (s.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]') OR fp.in_window > 0)

## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE f.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]')) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(f.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.tags) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?))) AND (f.id IN (SELECT fp.finding_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.place_id = ANY(?::text[])) OR f.region_name ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') AND (f.geom IS NOT NULL AND f.geom && ST_MakeEnvelope(?, ?, ?, ?, 4326))) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.authors) x WHERE x ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\') AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(s.topics) x WHERE lower(x) = lower(?)) AND lower(s.status) IN (?, ?) AND (s.year IS NOT NULL AND s.year BETWEEN ? AND ?) AND s.title ILIKE ? ESCAPE '\' AND s.citekey IN (SELECT ref FROM goal_links WHERE goal_slug = ? AND kind = 'source') AND fp.matched > 0 AND (s.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]') OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey

AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
