AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), I(0), I(0)
## countQuery
SELECT count(*)::int FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## findingsFeedQuery
SELECT f.citekey, s.title, f.claim, f.region_name, ST_X(f.geom), ST_Y(f.geom), lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, f.confidence, f.significance, s.doi, s.venue, s.source, s.tags, s.topics, EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM media_links m WHERE m.target_type = 'source' AND m.target_id = s.citekey AND m.role = 'cover') FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY lower(f.period) NULLS LAST, f.citekey, f.id LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(walls), S(walls), I(0), I(0)
## findingsCountQuery
SELECT count(*)::int FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## selectionCountsQuery
SELECT (SELECT count(*)::int FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM articles a WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article_links al WHERE al.src_slug = a.slug AND al.target_kind = 'source' AND al.target = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))))), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM events e WHERE (e.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM attestations ea WHERE ea.target_type = 'event' AND ea.target_id = e.id AND ea.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))))))
  params: S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)
## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)
## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S(walls), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)

## where
(s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## sourceKeys
SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## articleWhere
EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article_links al WHERE al.src_slug = a.slug AND al.target_kind = 'source' AND al.target = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))))
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## eventWhere
(e.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM attestations ea WHERE ea.target_type = 'event' AND ea.target_id = e.id AND ea.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))))))
  params: S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)
## sourceWhere
(s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(walls), S(walls)

SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S(walls), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)
# sort cited with q · scoped
## where
(s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## sourceKeys
SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## articleWhere
EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article_links al WHERE al.src_slug = a.slug AND al.target_kind = 'source' AND al.target = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))))
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## eventWhere
(e.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM attestations ea WHERE ea.target_type = 'event' AND ea.target_id = e.id AND ea.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))))))
  params: S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)

## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), I(0), I(0)

  params: S(walls), S(walls)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY lower(f.period) NULLS LAST, f.citekey, f.id LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(walls), S(walls), I(0), I(0)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY lower(s.period) ASC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY lower(s.period) ASC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S(walls), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)

  params: S(walls), S(walls)

## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY lower(s.period) ASC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls), I(0), I(0)

  params: S(walls), S(walls)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY s.year DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?

## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## periodsQuery findings

  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.year DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY s.year DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(walls), S(walls)
## snippetsQuery
SELECT sc.citekey, sc.heading, ts_headline('english', sc.body, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?), ?) AS snippet FROM source_chunks sc INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, ord FROM source_chunks WHERE citekey IN (?, ?) AND tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) * 0.001 ELSE ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) END DESC, ord) best ON best.citekey = sc.citekey AND best.ord = sc.ord
  params: S(walls), S(MaxFragments=1, MaxWords=30, MinWords=8, StartSel=**, StopSel=**, HighlightAll=false), S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b), S(walls), S(walls), S(walls)

OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY s.year ASC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?

AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY lower(s.title) ASC, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?

# limit offset with q · no scope
## where
(s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(tin), S(tin)
## sourceKeys
SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(tin), S(tin)
## articleWhere
EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article_links al WHERE al.src_slug = a.slug AND al.target_kind = 'source' AND al.target = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))))
  params: S(tin), S(tin)
## eventWhere
(e.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM attestations ea WHERE ea.target_type = 'event' AND ea.target_id = e.id AND ea.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))))))
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin)
## sourceWhere
(s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(tin), S(tin)
## findingPlaceWhere
TRUE
  params: 
## findingPeriodWhere
TRUE
  params: 
## findingWhere
TRUE
  params: 
## orderBySql
ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(tin)
## orderBySql cte
ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(tin)
## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), I(25), I(50)
## countQuery

  params: S(tin), S(tin)

AND TRUE), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM articles a WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article_links al WHERE al.src_slug = a.slug AND al.target_kind = 'source' AND al.target = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))))), (SELECT count(*)::int FROM events e WHERE (e.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM attestations ea WHERE ea.target_type = 'event' AND ea.target_id = e.id AND ea.citekey = ANY(ARRAY(SELECT s.citekey FROM sources s WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))))))))
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin)
## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: S(tin), S(tin)
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE
  params: S(tin), S(tin)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE TRUE) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND (TRUE OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) AND TRUE ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey

## sourcesQuery
WITH fts_m AS MATERIALIZED (SELECT citekey, ord, heading, ts_rank_cd(tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) AS r FROM source_chunks WHERE tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), best_chunks AS (SELECT DISTINCT ON (citekey) citekey, r AS rank FROM fts_m ORDER BY citekey, CASE WHEN heading ~* 'references|bibliography|notes|index|acknowledg' THEN r * 0.001 ELSE r END DESC, ord) SELECT s.citekey, s.title, s.year, s.status, s.topics, s.regions, lower(s.period), upper(s.period) - 1, s.summary, s.tags, s.authors, s.doi, (bc.citekey IS NOT NULL) AS body_hit, NULL::text AS snippet, NULL::text AS heading FROM sources s LEFT JOIN best_chunks bc ON bc.citekey = s.citekey WHERE (s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) ORDER BY (ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0)) DESC, s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey LIMIT ? OFFSET ?
  params: S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), S(tin), I(25), I(50)

## periodsQuery sources
SELECT COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(*) AS matched, count(*) FILTER (WHERE f.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]')) AS in_window FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND (s.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]') OR fp.in_window > 0)
  params: L(-1700), L(-1100), L(-1700), L(-1100)
## periodsQuery findings
SELECT lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1 FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND f.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]')
  params: L(-1700), L(-1100)
## treeRowsQuery sources
SELECT s.citekey, s.citekey, s.title, COALESCE(lower(s.period), fp.min_start), COALESCE(upper(s.period) - 1, fp.max_end), coalesce(fp.place_ids, '{}'::text[]) FROM sources s LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT min(lower(f.period)) AS min_start, max(upper(f.period) - 1) AS max_end, count(DISTINCT f.id) AS matched, count(DISTINCT f.id) FILTER (WHERE f.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]')) AS in_window, array_agg(DISTINCT l.place_id) FILTER (WHERE l.place_id IS NOT NULL) AS place_ids FROM findings f LEFT JOIN finding_places l ON l.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND TRUE) fp ON true WHERE TRUE AND (s.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]') OR fp.in_window > 0) ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: L(-1700), L(-1100), L(-1700), L(-1100)
## treeRowsQuery findings
SELECT f.id, f.citekey, s.title, lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f JOIN sources s ON s.citekey = f.citekey WHERE TRUE AND f.period && int8range(?::bigint, ?::bigint, '[]') ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
  params: L(-1700), L(-1100)
# facets · empty · no scope
## facetsQuery tags
SELECT mode() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY v) AS value, count(DISTINCT s.citekey)::int AS count, NULL::text AS place_id, FALSE AS contested FROM sources s CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT x FROM unnest(s.tags) x UNION SELECT y FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) y WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey) AS t(v) WHERE TRUE AND v IS NOT NULL AND v <> '' GROUP BY lower(v) ORDER BY count(DISTINCT s.citekey) DESC, lower(v) ASC LIMIT ?
  params: I(50)

ESCAPE '\' GROUP BY lower(v) ORDER BY count(DISTINCT s.citekey) DESC, lower(v) ASC LIMIT ?

## facetsQuery places
SELECT mode() WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY v) AS value, count(DISTINCT s.citekey)::int AS count, (SELECT pn.place_id FROM place_names pn JOIN places p ON p.id = pn.place_id WHERE lower(pn.name) = min(lower(v)) ORDER BY (lower(p.label) = min(lower(v))) DESC, p.id LIMIT 1) AS place_id, coalesce((SELECT (p.properties->>'contested')::boolean FROM place_names pn JOIN places p ON p.id = pn.place_id WHERE lower(pn.name) = min(lower(v)) ORDER BY (lower(p.label) = min(lower(v))) DESC, p.id LIMIT 1), false) AS contested FROM sources s CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT x FROM unnest(s.regions) x UNION SELECT f.region_name FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND f.region_name IS NOT NULL UNION SELECT p.label FROM findings f JOIN finding_places fp ON fp.finding_id = f.id JOIN places p ON p.id = fp.place_id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey) AS t(v) WHERE TRUE AND v IS NOT NULL AND v <> '' GROUP BY lower(v) ORDER BY count(DISTINCT s.citekey) DESC, lower(v) ASC LIMIT ?
  params: I(50)

# fragments
## tsQuery
websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)
  params: S(sea peoples)
## ftsPredicate
(s.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(sea peoples), S(sea peoples)
## ftsPredicate f
(f.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_chunks fts_p WHERE fts_p.citekey = f.citekey AND fts_p.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)))
  params: S(x), S(x)
## rankExpr
(ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce((SELECT max(ts_rank_cd(fts_r.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?))) FROM source_chunks fts_r WHERE fts_r.citekey = s.citekey AND fts_r.tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)), 0))
  params: S(sea peoples), S(sea peoples), S(sea peoples)
## rankExprCte
(ts_rank_cd(s.tsv, websearch_to_tsquery('english', ?)) + coalesce(bc.rank, 0))
  params: S(sea peoples)
## findingsQuery
SELECT f.citekey, f.id, f.claim, f.region_name, ST_X(f.geom), ST_Y(f.geom), lower(f.period), upper(f.period) - 1, f.period_name, f.tags, ARRAY(SELECT p.label FROM finding_places fp JOIN places p ON p.id = fp.place_id WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY p.label), ARRAY(SELECT fp.place_id FROM finding_places fp WHERE fp.finding_id = f.id ORDER BY fp.place_id) FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey IN (?, ?) ORDER BY f.citekey, f.id
  params: S(zqa1990_a), S(zqb2001_b)
## orderBy
relevance: ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
cited: ORDER BY s.cited_by_count DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
period: ORDER BY lower(s.period) ASC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
year: ORDER BY s.year DESC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
oldest: ORDER BY s.year ASC NULLS LAST, s.citekey
title: ORDER BY lower(s.title) ASC, s.citekey
## facetValues
tags: SELECT x FROM unnest(s.tags) x UNION SELECT y FROM findings f, unnest(f.tags) y WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey
authors: SELECT x FROM unnest(s.authors) x
places: SELECT x FROM unnest(s.regions) x UNION SELECT f.region_name FROM findings f WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey AND f.region_name IS NOT NULL UNION SELECT p.label FROM findings f JOIN finding_places fp ON fp.finding_id = f.id JOIN places p ON p.id = fp.place_id WHERE f.citekey = s.citekey
topics: SELECT x FROM unnest(s.topics) x
status: SELECT s.status
provenance: SELECT coalesce(s.source, 'unknown')
